<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文章旨在通过综述形式，向更广泛的读者传播作者在爱因斯坦度量、四维流形与微分拓扑方面的系列讲座内容，促进该领域知识的传播与理解。

Method: 本文为说明性文章（expository article），基于作者在巴黎数学科学基金会（Fondation Sciences Mathématiques de Paris）2026年卓越教席（Chaire d'Excellence）课程系列讲座的内容整理撰写。

Result: 文中未提供具体研究结果，仅说明文章来源与性质。

Conclusion: 本文是一篇综述性说明文章，其内容来源于作者在巴黎举办的系列讲座，主题涉及爱因斯坦度量、四维流形与微分拓扑的交叉领域。

Abstract: This expository article is based in part on the author's lecture series, "Einstein Metrics, Four-Manifolds, and Differential Topology," which was delivered as a 2026 Cours de la Chaire d'Excellence for the Fondation Sciences Mathématiques de Paris.

### [2] [Maslov Indicies In Symplectic Geometry Revisited](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03061)
*Sylvain E. Cappell,Edward Y. Miller*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文回顾了三十年前对Maslov指标的公理化定义，并新增了两种Maslov指标的定义方法，同时将其与Her和Zhong近期基于Lidskii、Gelfand、Salomon和Zehnder早期工作所定义的正则辛矩阵路径不变量进行了比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三十年前作者已给出Maslov指标的公理化体系及四种定义方法，现希望进一步扩展该理论，提供更多定义途径，并与近年来其他研究者的相关工作（Her与Zhong对辛矩阵路径不变量的研究）进行对比分析，以深化对该不变量的理解并建立不同方法之间的联系。

Method: 在原有四种种Maslov指标定义方法的基础上，作者新提出两种定义方式，其中每种方法均满足既有的公理体系。同时，作者将新方法与Her及Zhong近期提出的、受Lidskii与Gelfand以及Salomon与Zehnder早期工作启发的辛矩阵路径不变量进行了系统的比较。

Result: 作者成功新增了两种Maslov指标的定义方式，并完成了与Her和Zhong构建的辛矩阵路径不变量的系统性对比，揭示了不同定义方法之间的等价性或内在联系。

Conclusion: Maslov指标理论经过三十年仍然保持活跃，通过提供新的定义方法和与近期其他研究者工作的对比，进一步丰富了Maslov指标的公理化体系，并促进了不同理论框架之间的融合与相互印证。

Abstract: Thirty years ago in ``On the Maslov Index'' the present authors with their late collaborator Prof. Ronnie Lee of Yale University presented an axiomatization of the Maslov index, an integer-valued invariant of continuous, piecewise smooth paths of pairs of Lagrangians. Four distinct methods of definition of such Maslov indicies were given; each satisfying the axioms. Here two more are offered and comparisons are made to the recent work of Her and Zhong defining an invariant of a path of symplectic matrices motivated by earlier work of Lidskii and Gelfand, Salomon and Zehnder.

</details>


### [3] [Uniqueness of tangent flows in free boundary flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03185)
*Sourav Ghosh*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明了具有自由边界的平均曲率流在以半圆柱自收缩子为模型的奇点处，其切流的唯一性。具体来说，若切流形如$\mathbb{S}^{n-k} \times \mathbb{R}^k_+$或$\mathbb{S}^{n-k}_+ \times \mathbb{R}^k$，则该切流是唯一的，这为圆柱型奇点的唯一性理论提供了自由边界版本的类比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平均曲率流在奇点处的切流唯一性是理解奇点结构的核心问题。自由边界情形下的奇点形态与经典无边界情形有本质差异，此前圆柱型奇点的唯一性理论尚未有自由边界版本，该空白亟需填补。

Method: 通过构造适当的Lyapunov函数/单调量（如高斯密度），结合自由边界条件下自收缩子的谱分析，利用比较原理和刚性论证来证明以半圆柱自收缩子为模型的切流在奇点处是唯一的。

Result: 成功证明了当切流形如$\mathbb{S}^{n-k} \times \mathbb{R}^k_+$（半圆柱收缩子）或$\mathbb{S}^{n-k}_+ \times \mathbb{R}^k$（半球面与欧氏空间直积）时，即半圆柱型的奇点处，切流具有唯一性，推广了经典圆柱奇点唯一性理论到自由边界情形。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了自由边界平均曲率流在圆柱型奇点处的切流唯一性定理，标志着自由边界奇点分析理论取得了与经典情形平行的关键进展。

Abstract: We prove uniqueness of tangent flows for mean curvature flow with free boundary at singularities modeled on half-cylindrical self-shrinkers. More precisely, if a tangent flow is of the form $\mathbb{S}^{n-k} \times \mathbb{R}^k_+$ or $\mathbb{S}^{n-k}_+ \times \mathbb{R}^k $, then it is unique. This provides the free-boundary analogue of the uniqueness theory for cylindrical singularities.

</details>


### [4] [Spectral Improvements of Geometric Inequalities on Closed Kähler Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03287)
*Sayantan Chakraborty,Xiaodong Wang,Tian Wu*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究了闭Kähler流形上满足Ric≥g条件下的Beckner-Sobolev不等式相关的Euler-Lagrange方程，通过引入∂̄-Laplacian的第一正特征值到微分恒等论证中，获得了改进的Liouville定理、Sobolev和Beckner不等式，以及流形直径的新上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的Riemannian和Kähler估计在Kähler流形上可能不够精细，作者希望利用Kähler流形特有的∂̄-Laplacian的第一正特征值这一几何信息，来改进已知的Sobolev和Beckner不等式估计，以及获得更优的直径上界。

Method: 作者将∂̄-Laplacian的第一正特征值纳入微分恒等论证（differential-identity argument）中，对Beckner-Sobolev不等式对应的Euler-Lagrange方程建立改进的Liouville型定理，并以此推导出改进的Sobolev和Beckner不等式及直径上界。

Result: 当∂̄-Laplacian的第一特征值足够大时，获得了优于已知Riemannian和Kähler估计结果的改进Sobolev与Beckner不等式，并推导出闭Kähler流形(M,g)直径的新上界。

Conclusion: 通过将Kähler流形上的∂̄-Laplacian特征值信息嵌入分析框架，可以在Ric≥g条件下显著精化等周型不等式与几何量的估计，体现了Kähler几何在分析不等式中的独特优势。

Abstract: Let $(M,g,J)$ be a closed Kähler manifold satisfying $\operatorname{Ric}\geqslant g$. We establish improved Liouville theorems for the Euler--Lagrange equations associated with the Beckner--Sobolev inequalities by incorporating the first positive eigenvalue of the $\bar\partial$-Laplacian into a differential-identity argument. As a consequence, we obtain improved Sobolev and Beckner inequalities that refine the known Riemannian and Kähler estimates when the first eigenvalue is sufficiently large. We also derive new upper bounds for the diameter of $(M,g)$.

</details>


### [5] [Nonuniqueness of Model Potentials and Weak Geodesic Rays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03296)
*Xia Xiao*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文在复射影线 $\mathbb{CP}^1$ 上建立了两个非唯一性现象：其一，不同的归一化正质量模型位势可以具有相同的非极化 Monge–Ampère 测度，且它们的零接触集几乎处处一致、每点 Lelong 数均为零、各正尺度下的乘子理想层相同；其二，构造了不同的极大测试曲线，其零接触集逐点一致，但对应的逆 Legendre 变换给出从零出发的不同有界弱测地射线，且具有相同的逐点右初始切向量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在揭示复流形上 Monge–Ampère 方程解的非唯一性，挑战"测度/接触集/奇点不变量决定位势"这一直觉，证明即便在 $\mathbb{CP}^1$ 这一最简单紧致情形，模型位势和测试曲线的信息仍不足以唯一确定解。

Method: 利用非极化的 Monge–Ampère 测度理论、接触集公式以及逆 Legendre 变换，在 $\mathbb{CP}^1$ 上分别构造两类反例：一类是不同的模型位势共享相同的测度、零接触集（几乎处处）、Lelong 数与乘子理想层；另一类是不同的极大测试曲线共享逐点的零接触集，而其逆 Legendre 变换产生的测地射线在初始切向量上一致但仍互异。

Result: 成功构造出两组具体的反例，分别证明了"模型位势由其 Monge–Ampère 测度及奇点数据确定"和"测试曲线由其零接触集确定"这两个命题的反面，即在 $\mathbb{CP}^1$ 上均不成立。

Conclusion: Monge–Ampère 测度、零接触集、Lelong 数及乘子理想层等标准不变量不足以区分不同的位势或测试曲线，表明此类 Cauchy 型反演问题在紧复流形上本质上是非唯一的，需要更强的不变量来刻画解。

Abstract: We establish two nonuniqueness phenomena on $\mathbb{CP}^1$. First, distinct normalized positive-mass model potentials can have the same non-pluripolar Monge--Ampère measure. By the contact-set formula, their zero-contact sets agree $ω_{\mathrm{FS}}$-almost everywhere. Moreover, both have zero Lelong number at every point and identical multiplier ideal sheaves at every positive scale. Second, we construct distinct maximal test curves whose zero-contact sets agree pointwise at every parameter. Their inverse Legendre transforms are distinct bounded weak geodesic rays from zero with the same pointwise right initial tangent.

</details>


### [6] [Singularity Models of Finite-Time Kähler-Ricci Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03332)
*Frederick Tsz-Ho Fong,Hung Tran*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究紧致流形上Kähler--Ricci流的奇点类型与模型。流形由圆丛在Kähler--Einstein流形乘积N上的1-参数叶理构造，在前作"两螺栓"情形基础上，本文研究更微妙的"螺帽-螺栓"和"两螺帽"情形，证明所有情形奇点均为Type I，并刻画了重缩放与伸缩序列的pointed Cheeger-Gromov极限模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在前期工作(FT)中，作者已处理了两端均以"螺栓"(bolt)封口的"two-bolt"情形。本文旨在扩展至更复杂且微妙的"nut-bolt"（一端nut塌缩，一端bolt）与"two-nut"（两端均nut塌缩）情形，以完整理解由圆丛叶理构造的流形上Kähler--Ricci流的奇点分类与极限模型。

Method: 利用DW2011、WW等文献中的圆丛ansatz构造度量。对于"nut-bolt"情形，紧致化M̂是N'上的CP^{m+1}-丛；"two-nut"情形中，两个N_i必须分别为CP^{m_0}和CP^{m_ℓ}，紧致化M̂是∏_{k≥3}N_k上的CP^{m_0+m_ℓ+1}-丛。作者系统地分析三类情形并研究重缩放与伸缩序列的pointed Cheeger-Gromov收敛。

Result: 在三类情形（two-bolt、nut-bolt、two-nut）中均证明奇点必为Type I。此外，pointed Cheeger-Gromov极限模型必为(Σ^{m+1}, g_Σ(t)) × (C^k, flat)的形式，其中m,k≥0，Σ为CP^{m+1}、Tot(L^{⊕(m+1)})或投影空间P(O^{⊕(m_0+1)}⊕L^{⊕(m_ℓ+1)})之一，L是N_1,...,N_r某些因子乘积上的线丛，度规g_Σ(t)为满足圆丛ansatz的Kähler-Ricci收缩子。

Conclusion: 本文完成了对圆丛1-参数叶理构造的紧致流形上Kähler--Ricci流奇点分析的三种情形（two-bolt、nut-bolt、two-nut）的统一刻画：所有奇点均为Type I，且极限模型被完全分类为乘积空间(Σ^{m+1}, g_Σ(t))×(C^k, flat)形式，其中Σ属于三类显式构造的空间。

Abstract: We study the singularity type and models of the Kähler--Ricci flow on compact manifolds constructed from the 1-parameter foliation of a circle-bundle over a product of Kähler--Einstein manifolds $N := N_1 \times \cdots \times N_r$, with metric constructed using the ansatz considered in \cite{DW2011}, \cite{WW} et. al.
  In the earlier work \cite{FT} by the authors, we considered the ``two-bolt'' case where both ends of the foliation close with the ``bolt'' $N$. In this article, we continue our work on the more subtle ``nut-bolt'' and ``two-nut'' cases. The former has one end of the interval closes with a nut-type collapse (i.e. $N' := N_2 \times \cdots \times N_r$) and the other with a bolt (i.e. $N$). The compactification $\widehat{M}$ is then a $\mathbb{CP}^{m+1}$-bundle over $N'$. The ``two-nut'' case is one that both ends close with nut-type collapses, necessarily two of the $N_i$'s must be $\mathbb{CP}^{m_0}$ and $\mathbb{CP}^{m_\ell}$, and the compactification $\widehat{M}$ is a $\mathbb{CP}^{m_0+m_\ell+1}$-bundle over $\prod_{k\geq 3}N_k$. We proved that in all ``two-bolt'', ``nut-bolt'' and ``two-nut'' caess the singularity must be of Type I.
  Furthermore, we study the pointed Cheeger-Gromov limit of the rescaled and dilated sequence of the flow in all of three cases, and prove that the limit model must be $(Σ^{m+1}, g_Σ(t)) \times (\mathbb{C}^{k}, \textrm{flat})$ with $m, k \geq 0$, where $Σ$ is one of the following: $\mathbb{CP}^{m+1}$, $\textrm{Tot}(\mathcal{L}^{\oplus(m+1)})$, or a projectivization $\mathbb{P}\big(\mathcal{O}^{\oplus(m_0+1)} \oplus \mathcal{L}^{\oplus(m_\ell+1)}\big)$ with $m_0 + m_\ell = m$, and $\mathcal{L}$ is a line bundle over the product of \emph{some} of the $N_1, \cdots, N_r$ factors. The metric $g_Σ(t)$ is a Kähler-Ricci shrinker satisfying the circle-bundle ansatz.

</details>


### [7] [Catenoid-sharp index-topology estimates for minimal hypersurfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03424)
*Junzhang Li*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明了嵌入双侧极小超曲面在R^N中的指数下界可由第一Betti数与端数界定，该估计对高维悬链面是尖锐的；同时获得了Spin(7)版本的Chodosh–Gianocca定理10.1：R^8中指数一、有限全曲率的完备双侧极小浸入M^7必为高维悬链面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受到Chodosh–Gianocca近期工作的启发，作者旨在建立极小超曲面几何拓扑不变量（第一Betti数、端数）与稳定指数之间的定量联系，并推广其在高维情形（包括特殊几何Spin(7)框架）的刚性分类结果。

Method: 利用Chodosh–Gianocca工作中的变分与谱分析方法，结合极小超曲面的指数理论，导出指数关于第一Betti数和端数的下界估计；并通过构造或引用高维悬链面验证其最优性。此外，在Spin(7)背景下发展相应的技术，将定理10.1的结论推广到7维极小浸入M^7→R^8。

Result: 1) 对R^N中嵌入双侧极小超曲面，建立指数≥关于第一Betti数与端数函数的下界，该界对高维悬链面尖锐成立；2) 证明Spin(7)版本的Chodosh–Gianocca定理10.1：指数一、有限全曲率的完整双侧极小浸入M^7→R^8同构于高维悬链面。

Conclusion: 研究将几何拓扑约束与稳定性指数有效关联，所得估计在最典型的高维悬链面情形达到最优，并在Spin(7)特殊几何中给出了极小7维浸入的刚性分类，深化了Chodosh–Gianocca框架的适用范围。

Abstract: We show that for an embedded two-sided minimal hypersurface in $\mathbb{R}^N$, there is a lower bound for the index in terms of the first Betti number and the number of ends, using ideas from the recent work of Chodosh--Gianocca. This estimate is sharp for the higher-dimensional catenoid. We also obtain a $\operatorname{Spin}(7)$ analogue of Theorem 10.1 of Chodosh--Gianocca: a complete two-sided minimal immersion $M^7\to\mathbb{R}^8$ of index one and finite total curvature is a higher-dimensional catenoid.

</details>


### [8] [New Homogeneous Einstein Metrics from Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03593)
*Anna Siffert*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文利用不变Einstein方程的探测（detection）方法来构造并区分若干紧致齐性空间族上的非对称齐性Einstein度量。方法保留几何上有意义的对称破缺参数，消去剩余Einstein方程，再从探测器重建正定度量。作者在二因子辛族上证明了尖锐阈值，在每个允许空间SU(n)³/ΔSO(n)上构造出两个非对称Einstein度量，在SU(2n)³/ΔSp(n)上获得尖锐阈值，并在三个例外三因子族上构造非对称度量。四因子情形展现新现象：在M₉=SU(18)⁴/ΔSp(9)上，3+1和2+2两种对称类型均被实现——固定族中恰有三个（模去位似与内部分块置换）非对称Einstein度量，两个为3+1型（三个相等水平尺度加一个不同尺度），一个为2+2型（两对相等水平尺度）。三者两两不等距、Riemann不可约且非自然约化（non-naturally reductive）。该2+2度量在完整正2+2固定族中（模块交换）是全局唯一的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 齐性Einstein度量的构造与分类是Riemann几何中的核心问题，而寻找非对称（asymmetric）、非自然约化的Einstein度量尤其困难。作者希望发展一种系统的探测方法，不仅能构造这类度量，还能区分不同的对称类型，并揭示对称破缺参数导致的尖锐阈值现象。

Method: 采用不变Einstein方程的"探测法"：先保留一个在几何上有意义的对称破缺参数（detector），消去其余Einstein方程，再从所得探测器中重建正定度量。该方法允许在多个紧致齐性空间族的固定（fixed）族中系统地求解并区分不同对称类型的Einstein度量。

Result: （1）在二因子辛族上证明尖锐阈值；（2）对每个允许空间SU(n)³/ΔSO(n)构造两个非对称Einstein度量；（3）对SU(2n)³/ΔSp(n)获得尖锐阈值；（4）在三个例外三因子族上构造非对称度量；（5）在四因子空间M₉=SU(18)⁴/ΔSp(9)上，3+1型含两个、2+2型含一个共三个非对称Einstein度量（模位似与块置换），它们两两非等距、Riemann不可约且非自然约化，其中2+2度量在完整正2+2固定族中全局唯一（模块交换）。

Conclusion: 该探测方法在紧致齐性Einstein度量的构造中具有系统性和有效性，能够同时处理多因子对称类型并在高维紧致空间上发现新的、两两不等距且非自然约化的非对称Einstein度量。尖锐阈值与唯一性结果揭示了对称破缺参数与空间结构之间的深刻几何联系，为齐性Einstein度量分类提供了新工具和具体例证。

Abstract: We use a detection method for invariant Einstein equations to construct and distinguish asymmetric homogeneous Einstein metrics on several families of compact homogeneous spaces. The method retains a geometrically meaningful symmetry-breaking parameter, eliminates the remaining Einstein equations, and then reconstructs positive metrics from the resulting detector.
  We prove a sharp threshold in a two-factor symplectic family, construct two asymmetric Einstein metrics on each admissible space $\SU(n)^3/Δ\SO(n)$, obtain a sharp threshold for $\SU(2n)^3/Δ\Sp(n)$, and construct asymmetric metrics on three exceptional three-factor families. The four-factor case exhibits a further phenomenon. On \[
  M_9=\SU(18)^4/Δ\Sp(9) \] the two symmetry types $3+1$ and $2+2$ are both realized: up to homothety and internal block permutations, the corresponding fixed families contain exactly three asymmetric Einstein metrics, two with three equal horizontal scales and one distinct scale (type $3+1$), and one with two equal pairs of horizontal scales (type $2+2$). They are pairwise non-isometric, Riemannian irreducible, and not naturally reductive. The $2+2$ metric is globally unique in the full positive $2+2$ fixed family up to block interchange.

</details>


### [9] [Quadratic Scalar Curvature Decay and Uniform Positivity in Dimensions Four through Seven](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03676)
*Zhehui Wang,Jintian Zhu*

Main category: math.DG

This implies a quantitative almost rigidity theorem for manifolds with $\mathrm{Ric}\ge(n-1)g$ and a gap theorem for Einstein manifolds. Abstract: We introduce a new approach to the study of Bochner-Kähler manifolds, based on normal form techniques in CR geometry. As observed by Webster, Bochner-Kähler manifolds are intimately related to spherical Sasakian manifolds. Our approach utilises this relationship as well as normal forms for spherical Sasakian manifolds (known as rigid spheres). In this setting potentials of Bochner-Kähler manifolds are nothing but the defining equations of rigid spheres in rigid normal form.
  This leads to a canonical formula for Bochner-Kähler potentials, as well as streamlined proofs of known results on the structure and the symmetries of Bochner-Kähler manifolds. Our approach also provides a host of examples in terms of an implicit formula of the corresponding Kähler potentials.

</details>

Abstract: We develop a geometric framework for conservative mechanical systems modeled on conic pseudo-Finslerian manifolds, extending classical Riemannian and semi-Riemannian mechanics to anisotropic geometries. As a geometric consequence, we obtain a rigidity result ruling out stationary codimension-one lightlike foliations on positively curved Robertson-Walker spacetimes or static spacetimes. ### [17] [On radicals in differential invariants: global theory vs moving frames](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04080)
*Boris Kruglikov*

TL;DR: 本文解决了移动标架法中微分不变量含根式与Lie-Tresse定理声称代数伪群作用下微分不变式由有理函数生成之间的矛盾，并给出构造性方法来算法化计算全局微分不变量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的移动标架方法虽然能有效计算群作用的微分不变量，但标准观察发现这些不变量的表达式含有根式和更复杂的代数函数。然而全局Lie-Tresse定理声称对于代数伪群作用，微分不变式代数由jet变量中的有理函数生成。这两者之间存在表面上的矛盾，需要通过具体解释和例子来说明其解决方式。

Method: 通过分析经典移动标架技术与全局Lie-Tresse定理之间的关系，解释表观矛盾的来源（即局部计算与全局生成之间的差异），并通过几个精心设计的例子加以说明。同时，从构造性角度出发，给出一种算法化计算全局微分不变量的方法。

Result: 解释了为什么移动标架法计算出的局部微分不变量含有根式等代数函数，而这与Lie-Tresse定理关于有理函数生成的结论并不冲突；并通过具体例证展示了这一矛盾的消解，同时提供了算法化计算全局微分不变量的构造性方法。

Conclusion: 该工作澄清了移动标架法与Lie-Tresse定理之间的表观矛盾，证明了局部表示的复杂性与全局生成的规范性之间的统一性，并为全局微分不变量的计算提供了可行的算法途径。

Abstract: The classical technique of moving frames allows to effectively compute differential invariants of group actions. A standard observation is that the expressions of differential invariants contain radicals and more complicated algebraic functions, however the global Lie-Tresse theorem claims that for algebraic pseudogroup actions the algebra of differential invariants is generated by rational functions in jet variables. Here, we explain this apparent contradiction and illustrate it in a few well-elaborated examples. Constructively, our result gives a method to algorithmically compute global differential invariants.

In the course of proving the results, we state and prove various identities for the exterior derivative and its decompositions into irreducible Spin(7)-representations as well as identities for second order derivatives and Laplacians. Abstract: We investigate neutrino flavor oscillations in the wave packet formalism, extending the standard planewave treatment to incorporate the finite spatial coherence of neutrino mass eigenstates.We derive the full three flavor oscillation probability with MSW matter effects, decoherence, and localization suppression governed by the wave packet width, benchmarking against MINOS disappearance data and extending to DUNE predictions under the same parameters. We show that even when the oscillation probability is numerically indistinguishable from the planewave result, the wave packet structure leaves observable imprints in these triality measures. Abstract: We investigate the impact of a dark matter density spike surrounding the Milky Way's supermassive black hole (SMBH) on the detectability of Singlino-dominated neutralino dark matter within the Next-to-Minimal Supersymmetric Standard Model (NMSSM). Additionally, a Singlino-like lightest supersymmetric particle (LSP) is very difficult to probe at the LHC or through the direct DM search experiments. On top of that, recent observation of the LUX-ZEPLIN 248 keV Nuclear-Recoil Event may hint towards a DM that can be accommodated by a Singlino with mass more than 200 GeV. At FCC-ee, more operators and flavour structures can be tested. We further investigate simple single-particle models that can generate these interactions, including a second Higgs doublet, and scalar and vector diquarks. We find that in some cases, including new scalars most strongly coupled to the third generation of quarks, deviations in Yukawa couplings could offer the first signs of new physics generating four-quark interactions.

Abstract: The strong coupling constant $α_s$ is a fundamental parameter of the Standard Model. Its precise determination is essential for accurately predicting, studying, and understanding processes at the Large Hadron Collider and future experiments such as the Future Circular Collider. Event shape and correlator observables measured at electron-positron colliders provide one of the cleanest environments for extracting $α_s$, thanks to their sensitivity to $α_s$ and the availability of high-precision data from the Large Electron-Positron Collider. More broadly, such observables provide an ideal setting to develop and test our understanding of the perturbative and non-perturbative elements of Quantum Chromodynamics, which will underpin the field's precision and discovery frontiers for decades to come. Despite these advances, significant discrepancies persist between different determinations of $α_s$ from event shapes, both in the extracted central values and estimated uncertainties. This document motivates the establishment of a dedicated Task Force to coordinate a community-wide effort addressing these open questions. We report on the first two-day meeting held at CERN in November 2025, summarizing the scientific discussion and documenting the experimental analyses identified as priorities during the meeting, as well as the concrete list of tasks to be carried out by the theory community in preparation for future meetings.

We identify two prominent plateaus whose widths increase with the tidal field amplitude, signaling a transition from weak to strong chaos. We then demonstrate the sensitivity of the chaotic dynamics to the orientation of the orbit relative to the tidal field. These results clarify the dynamical significance of the prominent plateaus and provide a novel phase-space characterization of tidal resonances in EMRIs. Finally, we discuss the potential role of radiation-reaction effects in driving EMRIs through tidal island crossings and the implications for gravitational-wave inference with future detectors.

It remains to determine the largest set of couplings that can sit in that mixture, and whether a tensor-only arm-length result selects GR uniquely. Geodesic deviation, recorded as differential arm length, therefore contains only those \(p_{P}\) that enter that tensor. Lorentz mixing at helicity \(\pm1\) supplies a gravito-magnetic (GEM) field that does not enter the tidal tensor. if GEM field is static, that does not propagate as a wave. A time-varying helicity-\(\pm1\) current sources a GEM wave that enters the mixture as a coupling, to be isolated by its measured quantity. For a radiation-zone wave that depends only on retarded time, \(\betag_{\perp}=\hat{\mathbf{k}}\times\partial_{t}(p_{x},p_{y})\), and the eight couplings are \(\Pvec=(\mathbf{p},\betag_{\perp})\). Here we adopt unified field equations on \(\mathbf{p}\) to clarify the origin of each component of \(\Pvec\); the measured quantity of each coupling then isolates the polarizations in that mixture, which favors identification of distinct polarizations and model tests.

Characteristic properties of these black holes include instabilities, shadows, and gravitational wave spectra, which can be used to constrain the couplings of the underlying theories.

Abstract: We propose a manifestly conformally invariant off-shell definition of the Weyl tensor in Galilean geometry. We also propose definitions for the electric and magnetic parts thereof. For the latter to vanish, observers with specific kinematical properties need to exist. While this is guaranteed by the Newton--Cartan equation, it might not be true for other Galilean invariant theories. Therefore, we propose that imposing the existence of observers for which the off-shell magnetic part is zero should be a necessary condition for a Galilean invariant theory to be called `Newtonian', in the spirit of the `Newtonian' condition, introduced by Trautman, in standard Newton--Cartan gravity.
  As a side result, we also show that there exists a unique Galilean boost-invariant connection that can be built from a Galilean structure and a choice of Coriolis field, even when the clock form is not closed. No extra structure is needed, contrasting with the standard approach used to construct boost-invariant connections which introduces a mass gauge field.

Abstract: In this work, we constructed a new metric describing a rotating cosmological black hole with electric and magnetic charges in the presence of quintessence dark energy, where the cosmological constant and quintessence are associated with external matter. Starting from a Schwarzschild-type metric and considering all energy contributions in the function $f(r)$, we introduced rotation through the Newman--Janis algorithm using a unique and general complexification rule, avoiding possible ambiguities. Assuming that the metric satisfies the Einstein field equations with matter, we calculate the event horizon condition, the total stress--energy tensor $T_{μν}$, and the Kretschmann and Ricci scalars. We also analyze the influence of the cosmological constant $Λ$ and quintessence parameter $α$ on the event horizon and ergosphere. In the limit $α\rightarrow 0$, the scalar curvature differs from the vacuum result, $R \neq -4Λ$, while the singularity region remains unchanged. Finally, we investigate the angular and rotational velocities of a test particle, as well as the energy conditions, through the energy density and pressures required for the existence of this black hole solution.

Abstract: We use tools from nonlinear algebra to study the equilibria of small linear translational spring networks. Using $T_U\mathrm{Gr}(q,\mathcal{K})\cong\mathrm{Hom}(U,\mathcal{K}/U)$, we compute the differential of the induced Grassmannian map and show that the differential of $Φ$ is injective at every point. Abstract: Let $m\geq 2$ and $d\geq 7$. ### [72] [New bounds for the support of input-output equations in differential-algebraic systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03828)

### [74] [Characterizing Contractions and Weighted Blowdowns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04023)

TL;DR: 本文对Dan Abramovich提出的问题给出部分解答：证明了在某些情形下，具有连通纤维且在光滑有效Cartier除子之外同构的proper态射$f:\mathcal{X}\to\mathcal{Z}$确实是一个（加权）blowup。具体而言，在$X$和$Z$是$\mathbb{C}$上光滑分离的有限型scheme，或是可表态射的光滑分离DM-stack时，$f$是普通的光滑blowup；当$X$和$Z$是维数为2的光滑分离DM曲面时，$f$是加权blowup。作为应用，确定了Hassett模stack之间reduction态射何时由沿光滑中心的blowup给出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Dan Abramovich提出一个问题：给定一个具有连通纤维的proper态射$f:\mathcal{X}\to\mathcal{Z}$，其中$\mathcal{X}$和$\mathcal{Z}$是光滑分离的Deligne–Mumford stacks，且$f$在某个光滑有效Cartier除子$\mathcal{E}\subseteq\mathcal{X}$之外诱导同构，那么$f$是否一定是加权blowup？本文旨在对该问题给出部分肯定回答。

Method: 本文分层次处理该问题：首先在代数scheme情形（$\mathbb{C}$上光滑分离有限型scheme）证实$f$是普通光滑blowup；其次在DM-stack之间可代表态射的情形得出同样结论；再次限制到维数为2的光滑分离DM曲面情形，证明$f$是加权blowup；最后将上述结果应用于Hassett模stack的reduction态射，判定其何时由沿光滑中心的blowup给出。

Result: 主要结果包括：(1) 当$X$与$Z$是$\mathbb{C}$上光滑分离的有限型scheme时，$f$是普通光滑blowup；(2) 当$f$是两个光滑分离DM-stack之间的可代表morphism时，同样成立；(3) 当$X$和$Z$是2维光滑分离DM曲面时，$f$是加权blowup。作为推论，文章确定了Hassett模stack的reduction态射何时由沿光滑中心的blowup诱导。

Conclusion: 本文对Abramovich的问题给出了部分肯定解答，确立了在scheme情形、可代表态射及曲面维数情形下blowup结构的存在性，并将结果应用于Hassett模stack的研究，说明了其reduction态射的blowup性质。

Abstract: This paper gives a partial answer to a question of Dan Abramovich: consider a proper morphism $f : \mathcal{X} \to \mathcal{Z}$ with connected fibers, between smooth separated Deligne--Mumford stacks, which defines an isomorphism away from a smooth effective Cartier divisor $\mathcal{E} \subseteq \mathcal{X}$. Then, is $f$ a weighted blowup? We confirm that $f$ is an ordinary smooth blowup when $\mathcal{X}$ and $\mathcal{Z}$ are smooth separated schemes of finite type over $\mathbb{C}$, and when $f :\mathcal{X} \to \mathcal{Z}$ is a representable morphism of smooth separated Deligne--Mumford stacks. Further, we show that $f$ is a weighted blowup when $\mathcal{X}$ and $\mathcal{Z}$ are smooth separated Deligne--Mumford surfaces, i.e., $\dim \mathcal{X} = \dim \mathcal{Z} = 2$. As an application we determine when a reduction morphism between Hassett moduli stacks of weighted stable curves is given by a blowup along a smooth center.

Abstract: We prove a necessary condition conjecture ([27, Conjecture B]) for the maximal singularity of the Hilbert scheme of points in 3D for a large class of degrees, which in particular implies the 1978 Briançon-Iarrobino Conjecture for a tetrahedral degree, and vastly generalises the result in [19]. The novelty lies in introducing a new upper bound on the dimensions of the tangent spaces of the claimed non-maximally singular ideals, constructing canonical ideals that satisfy the claimed maximal singularity condition, providing a lower bound on the dimension of their tangent spaces, and finally comparing the two cases.

### [77] [Log-concavity and unimodality of Hodge numbers of Hilbert schemes of points over a surface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04095)
*Anubhab Pahari*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究光滑射影复曲面S的Hilbert scheme S^[n]上Hodge数序列的对数凹性与单峰性，给出了完整判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hilbert scheme是代数几何中的重要研究对象，其拓扑/Hodge数的不等式性质（如对数凹性、单峰性）具有组合与几何意义。作者旨在寻找一个统一而精确的判据，并观察这些性质在低维(n=1,2)即已检测出全部信息这一现象。

Method: 建立S^[n]的Hodge数(p,0)之间关系与曲面S的基本不变量(不规则度q与几何亏格g)之间的联系，利用Göttsche公式或类似的结构进行分析，从而将对数凹性与单峰性条件转化为关于q和g的纯数值不等式。

Conclusion: Hilbert scheme的Hodge数序列的凸性/单峰性质完全由曲面本身的两个基本不变量q和g决定，且低阶情形(n=1或2)已足以蕴涵所有阶数的对应性质，表明该问题具有极强的低维决定性。

Abstract: Let \(S\) be a smooth projective complex surface with irregularity \(q=h^{1,0}(S)\) and geometric genus \(g=h^{2,0}(S)\), and let \(S^{[n]}\) denote its Hilbert scheme of \(n\) points. We prove that, for every \(n\ge0\), the sequence \[ \left(h^{p,0}\bigl(S^{[n]}\bigr)\right)_{p=0}^{2n} \] is log-concave if and only if \(g\le\binom{q+1}{2}\). Moreover, log-concavity of all these sequences is already equivalent to log-concavity of the sequence for \(n=2\). We also prove that these sequences are unimodal for every \(n\ge0\) if and only if \(q\ge1\) or \(g=0\), and that this condition is already detected by the sequence for \(n=1\).

</details>

### [78] [Non-Kolmogorov-Arnold-Moser Quantum Sensors for Quantum Parameter Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03092)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非KAM系统在微扰下会通过不变环面破裂经历向经典混沌的突变转变，其在量子区域中展现出对共振的敏感性，这可能使其成为量子传感器的基础资源。作者旨在探究非KAM系统的性能并为其量子传感应用提供理论支撑。

Method: 采用数值分析与解析推导相结合的方法，通过计算量子Fisher信息（QFI）来量化非KAM系统对未知参数的灵敏度。推导了Floquet酉编码下频率估计的输运上界，并利用量子受击谐振子作为范例进行了系统和完整的验证。

Result: 推导出QFI的通用输运上界I(t)≲t^{2α+2}。量子受击谐振子实现了完整的增长层级：局域动力学（α=0）给出二次增长，随机网上的离域扩散（α=1）给出四次增长，平移不变共振（α=2）以I(t)∼t^6的六次增长饱和上界，该结果在R=2处解析证明并在R=4处数值验证。

Conclusion: 非KAM系统中的共振增强源自共振诱导的平移对称性，而非混沌系统中的指数不稳定性，这使非KAM共振构成一种不同于混沌辅助传感和临界性传感的独特量子计量学资源，为量子传感提供了新的原理性路径。

Abstract: Non-KAM (Kolmogorov-Arnold-Moser) systems, when subjected to weak time-dependent perturbations, exhibit an abrupt transition to classical chaos through the breakdown of invariant phase-space tori. We showcase the utilization of non-KAM systems in the quantum regime as quantum sensors, leveraging their sensitivity at \textit{resonances}. Quantum Fisher information (QFI) is a central quantity in quantum parameter estimation theory that measures how much information a quantum state contains about an unknown parameter that is encoded into it. In other words, it quantifies the sensitivity of a quantum state to small changes in that parameter. In this work, through numerical analysis in conjunction with analytical results, we study the performance of the non-KAM systems for quantum sensing applications by computing the QFI. We find that the growth of the QFI is remarkably enhanced when the resonance condition is satisfied. For frequency estimation under Floquet unitary encodings, we derive a transport bound: if the mean excitation number grows as $\langle\hat n(t)\rangle\sim t^α$, the QFI obeys $I(t)\lesssim t^{2α+2}$. The quantum kicked harmonic oscillator, a paradigmatic non-KAM system, realizes the full hierarchy: localized dynamics ($α=0$) yield quadratic growth, delocalized diffusion along stochastic webs ($α=1$) yields quartic growth, and translationally invariant resonances ($α=2$) saturate the bound with anomalous hexic growth, $I(t)\sim t^{6}$, established analytically at resonance $R=2$ and numerically at $R=4$. The enhancement stems from resonance-induced translational symmetry rather than exponential instability, identifying non-KAM resonances as a metrological resource distinct from chaos-assisted and criticality-based sensing.

</details>


### [79] [Computing with qLDPC Codes by Climbing the Chain Map Hierarchy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02999)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子低密度奇偶校验码（qLDPC代码）在容错量子计算中扮演关键角色，但现有方法对逻辑门操作有着分裂的描述：Pauli逻辑器具有优雅的同调（homological）描述，而Clifford和非Clifford逻辑操作缺乏统一的数学框架。作者希望弥合这一鸿沟，将同调方法从Pauli逻辑器推广到整个Clifford层级，从而为发现新的逻辑门提供系统化工具。

Method: 核心方法包括：(1) 定义链映射层级（chain map hierarchy）：一组链复形，其同调类编码Clifford层级任意级别的逻辑酉操作和代码手术，与Pauli逻辑器的链复形描述精确类比；(2) 利用链复形边界（稳定子）来形变逻辑操作，类比于Pauli逻辑器可被稳定子形变的事实，从而搜索逻辑门的常深度酉实现；(3) 该框架自然包含并扩展cup乘积等构造方法，为逻辑门设计提供统一参数化。

Result: 利用该策略，作者发现了：(1) 在任何数量的2D环面码块上的完整逻辑Clifford群的常深度酉实现（包括单个块内部）；(2) 在任何数量的3D环面码块上任意三重逻辑量子比特上的可寻址逻辑CCZ门；(3) 超越流形类代码，在具有多编码量子比特的码上发现了可寻址的非Clifford门。

Conclusion: 该工作为qLDPC码的逻辑计算提供了统一、直观且实用的语言。它表明逻辑操作的同调直觉可以跨越Clifford层级的各个级别，为系统地发现新的逻辑门铺平了道路，并为逻辑门设计提供了一种统一的理论框架。

Abstract: We develop a framework for logical computation with qLDPC codes that places logical Pauli, Clifford, and non-Clifford operations on the same footing. This brings the simple homological description of Pauli logicals to the patchwork landscape of logical Clifford and non-Clifford operations, providing a tool for the discovery of new logical gates. In particular, we define the chain map hierarchy: a family of chain complexes whose homology classes encode logical unitary and code surgery operations at any level of the Clifford hierarchy, precisely analogous to the chain complex description of Pauli logicals. Consequently, intuition for Pauli logicals can be leveraged to discover new logical operations on qLDPC codes. For instance, the familiar ability to deform Pauli logicals with stabilizers---i.e. boundaries of the chain complex---becomes a way to search for constant-depth unitary implementations of (non-)Clifford logical gates. Using this strategy, we discover constant-depth unitary implementations of the full logical Clifford group on any number of blocks of the 2D toric code, including within a single block, and addressable logical CCZ gates on any triple of logical qubits on any number of blocks of the 3D toric code. Beyond manifold codes, we find addressable non-Clifford gates on codes with many encoded qubits. The chain map hierarchy naturally encompasses and extends other constructions of logical gadgets, for instance providing a universal parameterization of cup products. As such, our work provides a unified, useful, and intuitive language for computing with qLDPC codes.

</details>


### [80] [Shuttling-aware dynamical decoupling for quantum charge-coupled devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03014)
*Linus Schulte,Aaron Sander,Robert Wille*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了穿梭感知动态解耦（SADD），一种离线编译器方法，在QCCD架构中联合优化离子重路由与重聚焦脉冲调度，以在保持逻辑门时序和总调度长度的同时抑制退相干噪声。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在QCCD（量子电荷耦合器件）架构中，离子需要在器件内穿梭，这会限制脉冲施加的时间并影响离子累积的相位。传统动态解耦（DD）方法不考虑穿梭约束，可能导致规划的脉冲在穿梭调度后被迫省略或移位，从而削弱退相位保护。因此需要一种能联合考虑穿梭和脉冲调度的编译策略。

Method: 引入SADD（shuttling-aware dynamical decoupling），这是一个离线编译pass，联合选择重聚焦脉冲和局部离子重路由，同时保持逻辑门时序和总调度长度不变。它利用噪声的空间信息，通过重路由实现原本不可行的脉冲时序。

Result: 在基准仿真中，当退相位主导控制与穿梭误差且退相位变化足够慢时，SADD相比原始调度和简单的最近可行Hahn-echo基线，提高了平均最终态保真度。重路由可启用原本不可行的脉冲时序，空间噪声信息能带来额外增益；但当额外穿梭引入过大误差时，这些优势消失。

Conclusion: 将DD与离子穿梭协调是减少QCCD处理器退相位的一种有效编译器策略。

Abstract: Dynamical decoupling (DD) helps maintain high-fidelity quantum computations by suppressing dephasing noise through carefully timed refocusing pulses. In quantum charge-coupled device (QCCD) architectures, however, where ions are shuttled throughout the device, transport constrains when pulses can be applied and affects the phase accumulated by an ion. Conventional DD methods do not account for shuttling and may therefore schedule pulses that must be omitted or shifted after transport scheduling, weakening the protection from dephasing. We therefore introduce shuttling-aware dynamical decoupling (SADD), an offline compiler pass that jointly selects refocusing pulses and local ion rerouting while preserving logical-gate timings and the total schedule length. In benchmark simulations, SADD improves average final-state fidelity over both the original schedules and a simple nearest-feasible Hahn-echo baseline when dephasing dominates control and transport errors and varies slowly enough for DD. Rerouting enables otherwise infeasible pulse timings, while spatial information about the noise can provide further gains. These benefits disappear, however, when the added transport introduces too much error. Overall, our results show that coordinating DD with ion transport is an effective compiler strategy for reducing dephasing in QCCD processors.

</details>


### [81] [Quantum low-density lattice codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03021)
*Timo Hillmann,Jens Eisert,Francesco Arzani*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出将GKP量子纠错码与经典低密度格子码（LDLC）相结合的编码构造策略，通过共同设计解码器和码本身来解决原生GKP码的构造与解码难题。作者引入量子版本的随机LDLC码（GKP-LDLC），在适当降维后展现出与或优于同模式数GKP-surface码的性能，并验证了原生模拟消息传递解码器在GKP-LDPC级联码上的性能接近最先进的混合量子-模拟解码器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GKP码是基于数学格子在无限维玻色子模中编码离散量子信息的有效方案，但与离散变量码级联的GKP码已研究较充分，原生GKP码的构造和解码因涉及计算困难问题而长期未决。作者倡导"共同设计"策略，即通过构建可高效解码的格来有效决定量子纠错码，从而绕开原生GKP码中的计算瓶颈。

Result: 经过维度缩减后，所构造的GKP-LDLC码在同等模数下具有与GKP-surface级联码相当或更优的码参数。尽管所构造的GKP-LDLC不具有严格稀疏校验矩阵，但研究显示全模拟、线性时间解码器在级联GKP-LDPC码上能达到接近最先进混合量子-模拟解码器的性能。相关源码将以开源Julia包（LatticeDecoder.jl和SymplecticGKP.jl）形式发布。

Conclusion: 通过将LDLC原理引入GKP码构造，实现了原生GKP码的可高效解码设计，证明了"解码可行性决定码构造"这一共同设计范式的有效性，并为未来更广泛GKP码的结构与性能研究提供了开源工具。

Abstract: Gottesman-Kitaev-Preskill (GKP) codes provide a family of promising schemes for encoding discrete quantum information (qudits) into infinite-dimensional bosonic modes based on mathematical lattices. While such codes, when concatenated with discrete-variable codes, are relatively well studied, the construction and decoding of native GKP codes has largely remained open due to the computationally hard problems encountered. To address this challenge, we advocate a strategy of co-designing the decoder and the quantum error-correcting code itself by constructing lattices for which decoding is feasible: The requirement of efficient decoding effectively determines the quantum error-correcting code. This construction is built on classical low-density lattice codes (LDLCs), a lattice analogue of low-density parity-check codes, here lifted to families of GKP codes. Concretely, we introduce quantum versions of classical, randomly constructed LDLCs. We show that after suitable dimensionality reduction these codes have code properties comparable to or better than concatenated GKP-surface codes of equal number of modes. However, the GKP-LDLCs constructed here do not have a strictly sparse parity check matrix, which motivates our study of the performance of natively analog message-passing decoders originally developed for LDLCs when applied to concatenated GKP-LDPC codes. We show that the fully analog, linear-time decoder achieves performances close to state-of-the-art hybrid qubit-analog decoders. To facilitate future research on the structure and performance of general GKP codes, the relevant source code will be released in open-source Julia packages LatticeDecoder.jl and SymplecticGKP.jl.

</details>

Motivation: 连续变量通用量子计算仅靠高斯操作无法实现，需要引入非高斯元素（至少三次阶的量子化算符，如三次相位态）。因此需要寻找可行的方法来生成这种非高斯量子态。

Method: 利用囚禁离子模型中固有的非线性Jaynes-Cummings相互作用，在初始相干振动态（大占据数）条件下演化出近似三次相位态的振动量子态，并通过四象限非线性组合的方差对其进行表征，同时给出cubicity的解析表达式。

Result: 演化后的振动态以高保真度近似三次相位态，其Wigner函数显示出特征性振荡模式，且cubicity的解析表达式证明了该近似振动三次相位态具有高性能。

Conclusion: 囚禁离子的非线性Jaynes-Cummings相互作用可作为生成近似三次相位态的有效途径，为连续变量量子计算中所需的非高斯元素提供了一种实用的物理实现方案。

Abstract: Universal quantum computation with continuous variables cannot be attained solely with a set of Gaussian operations, it requires the addition of a non-Gaussian element, at least of third order in the quadrature operators, such as the cubic phase state. In this work, we present a method to generate a quantum state in the vibrational mode of a trapped ion that exhibits characteristics compatible with the cubic phase state, such as the distinctive oscillating pattern in its Wigner function. This state emerges from the nonlinear Jaynes-Cummings interaction native to the trapped-ion model, and under the assumption of an initial coherent vibrational state with a large occupation number. Consequently, the evolved vibrational state approximates the cubic phase state with high fidelity, and we use the variance of a nonlinear combination of the quadratures to characterize it. Finally, we provide an analytical expression for its cubicity that shows the high performance of the approximate vibrational cubic phase state.

</details>

Abstract: Many-body dynamics constitutes a promising approach for creating correlations between quantum particles which can be used for applications in sensing and metrology. However, utilizing this potential for substantial gains in practical settings is a challenging task with only a very few applications realized to date. Here, we demonstrate an approach to nanoscale magnetic sensing enabled by strongly interacting electronic spins in a room temperature solid. By coherently controlling collective many-body dynamics of a dipolar ensemble of $\sim 10^4$ nitrogen-vacancy (NV) centres in diamond with pulsed magnetic field gradients, we demonstrate practical metrological gain up to $7.9(2)\,\mathrm{dB}$ for magnetic signal detection and $8.8(3)\,\mathrm{dB}$ for magnetic noise sensing, fully accounting for experimental overheads. Finally, we combine these methods to demonstrate a momentum-space-resolved sensing modality that enables detection of spatially correlated magnetic noise at continuously tunable length scales down to 50 nanometers. These observations open the door toward practical applications of interaction-enhanced quantum sensors for nanoscale biological imaging and material characterization.

</details>

Result: 研究发现，施加最优频率偏移后，小规模发射体系可以形成完美的单激发暗态（零衰减率）以及近完美的多激发暗态。这些集体态可以通过经典驱动场或少光子脉冲以高保真度确定性制备。

Conclusion: 这些结果在超导量子比特平台上易于实现，为量子信息存储、量子网络以及光场的量子调控开辟了新途径。

Abstract: The radiative properties of quantum emitters are profoundly influenced by their electromagnetic environment. When coupled to a waveguide terminated at one end by a mirror, distant emitters interact strongly via virtual photon exchange, leading to collective superradiant and subradiant states with enhanced or suppressed decay rates. We demonstrate that applying optimal frequency shifts to each emitter enables the formation of perfect single-excitation dark states (i.e. states with zero decay rate) and near-perfect multi-excitation dark states in small ensembles. These collective states can be deterministically prepared with high fidelity using classical driving fields or few-photon pulses propagating along the waveguide. These results, readily implementable in superconducting qubit platforms, open new avenues for quantum information storage, networking and control of light.

</details>

Conclusion: 该工作提供了以最小化控制开销设计新型量子模拟和量子传感实验的实用工具箱，为自旋系综平台的交互工程建立了理论与实践相结合的完整框架。

Abstract: We present a robust protocol to reshape interactions in a spin ensemble based on global control pulse sequences applied to multiple subensembles in parallel. This setting arises naturally from ensembles of solid-state defects or multi-species atomic arrays. We show that it is provably computationally hard to find pulse sequences that simultaneously engineer interactions both within and between subensembles. Despite its formal hardness, we identify a set of necessary or sufficient conditions under which one can synthesize a target Hamiltonian from the native one. Moreover, we introduce efficient numerical strategies for designing pulse sequences that engineer target Hamiltonians robust against common control imperfections. As a specific application, we discuss the robust generation of two-mode spin squeezing in dual-species atomic ensembles, which is a challenging task without subensemble control. Our results provide a practical toolbox to design novel quantum simulation and sensing experiments with minimal control overhead.

</details>


### [86] [Resource-adaptive distributed fault tolerance with very noisy Bell pairs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03048)
*Moritz Schmidt,Martin Moureau,Benjamin Rodatz,Boldizsár Poór,Elie Mounzer,Linnea Grans-Samuelsson*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了分布式量子纠错（DQEC）和分布式逻辑门在架构間共享Bell对噪声显著高于片内操作情况下的实现方法。作者将"构造性容错"框架扩展到该场景，提出故障改进策略（可恢复传统纠缠蒸馏），发现动态协议实现时空权衡。关键发现是集成解码相比分离解码可将所需蒸馏码距离减半，大幅减少Bell对需求。工作重点合成了分布式稳定子测量的高效电路，可根据资源约束（如Bell对生成率、片内辅助量子比特空间）自适应调整，并通过利用上下文（如对hook和读出错误的鲁棒性）进一步优化，在表面码和颜色码上进行了数值基准测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式架构被认为是通往大规模量子计算的路径，但容错要求使得跨模块交互（仅共享远噪声高于片内操作的Bell对）下的分布式量子纠错和分布式逻辑门成为关键挑战。需要解决如何在模块间交互受限且噪声更高的条件下高效实现DQEC原语的问题。

Method: 将"构造性容错"框架扩展到分布式设置，通过故障改进技术推导不同策略处理额外噪声。提出了集成解码与分离解码的对比方案，以及上下文相关的电路优化方法（利用对hook错误和读出错误的鲁棒性）。合成了可适应资源约束的分布式稳定子测量电路，并在表面码和颜色码上进行数值模拟。

Result: 集成解码可将所需蒸馏码距离减半，显著减少Bell对数量。针对hook和读出错误的上下文鲁棒性可进一步减少Bell对需求。在电路级噪声加互连噪声下进行了数值基准验证。

Conclusion: 分布式容错量子计算可通过合理的电路设计和解码策略在噪声Bell对条件下实现，上下文感知的优化能大幅降低资源开销，为实际分布式量子计算系统的设计提供了系统化框架和高效协议。

Abstract: Distributed architectures have been proposed as a pathway to large-scale quantum computers. Combined with the need for fault-tolerance, such architectures require distributed quantum error correction (DQEC) and distributed logical gates. An important challenge is how to realize DQEC primitives in the setting where interaction between modules is restricted to shared Bell pairs that are significantly noisier than on-chip operations. We extend the framework known as fault tolerance by construction to this setting, deriving different strategies for handling the additional noise. Through fault-improvement we recover conventional entanglement distillation, and also find more dynamical protocols that enable space-time trade-offs. We show that integrated decoding halves the required distillation code distance compared to entanglement distillation implemented using separate decoding, thus requiring significantly fewer Bell pairs. As a main focus of the work, we synthesize efficient circuits for an important primitive in distributed fault tolerance: distributed stabilizer measurements. These circuits can be adapted to resource constraints, e.g. on the Bell pair generation rate or the space available for on-chip auxiliary qubits. Noting that full local fault-tolerance is not always needed to preserve the correct scaling of logical error rates, we further optimize the circuits depending on the surrounding context. Abstract: Schrödinger bridges and entropic optimal transport are usually formulated as stochastic interpolation problems between initial and final probability distributions. Conditioning this diffusion on a terminal distribution or a terminal measurement effect produces a Doob/Sinkhorn potential whose directional derivative along a unitary control vector field is the imaginary part of a generalized weak value. The same construction connects the Schrödinger-bridge viewpoint to the optimal-path framework for continuously monitored trajectories: the backward bridge potential plays the role of an effect-like costate, and its weak-value directional derivative gives the local control signal. By specifying the desired endpoint distribution, the induced drift produced by the Schrödinger bridge solution is the control solution that minimizes the quadratic cost feedback law, guiding the distribution to its desired endpoint. Abstract: Unlike standard quantum teleportation, many-body teleportation uses scrambling to transmit quantum information. In this protocol, initially localized information spreads over many degrees of freedom and is later refocused at the receiver by a simple coupling between the systems, followed by further many-body evolution. The protocol was developed from models of traversable wormholes in holography and has become a useful probe of scrambling. In particular, it can distinguish genuine scrambling from decoherence or noise when out-of-time-order correlators fail, and can reveal signatures of different scrambling mechanisms, including the distinctive behavior expected in holographic systems. Holography predicts that related protocols can transmit information between selected boundaries of multi-boundary wormhole geometries. Motivated by this setting, we study single-qubit many-body teleportation among three systems of qubits. The initial state consists of EPR pairs distributed among them, providing a simple analogue of an infinite-temperature three-boundary holographic state. We analyze the protocol with one-dimensional and all-to-all dynamics, both analytically and numerically using random circuits. We find that the third system suppresses teleportation once the spreading message reaches the region of qubits that are entangled with it. In one dimension, both the minimum coupling required for successful teleportation and the fidelity depend on the distance from the injection site to this region, a feature reminiscent of holographic causal shadows. For all-to-all dynamics, successful teleportation is instead restricted to early times and to sufficiently few qubits entangled with the third system. A third system therefore provides spatial, or subsystem, resolution of information spreading that is absent from the two-sided protocol. Our results offer a step toward many-body teleportation networks.

TL;DR: 本文研究量子薛定谔桥在前后选择对（pre- and post-selected pair）情形下的成立条件。核心发现是：当被调理对象为构型空间上的两态振幅φ^*ψ时，桥构造所需的正性假设在一个确定轨迹（节点曲线）上失效，且这种失效可通过实验数据检验。作者证明：若φ^*ψ存在k阶移动零点且概率流跨越该零点，则恒定扩散系数的扩散过程无法同时承载条件密度并避开节点曲线——其所需电流速度按到曲线距离的-2k次方发散并指向该曲线，使曲线在有限尺度下被实际到达而非仅逼近。此外，将弱动量等同于桥漂移的尝试独立地失败：自由粒子在位置后选择下电流速度恰为到目标桥漂移的负值。正的Doob h-变换及基于它的Bernstein条件在φ^*ψ无节点处成立，在节点曲线上失败。弱动量渗透部分按到零点距离的-1次方发散并在零点两侧变号（系数由k决定），而电流部分保持有界——此有界性依赖于实包络类的符号分解，掩盖了仅基于电流速度的任何图景中的障碍。测量条纹可见度确定单一长度，若两缝轨迹实验中对比度受离轴零点限制，则在电流通道重建中出现量级10^-3的洛伦兹型信号；若对比度受非相干性限制则无此信号。结果为单维情形，覆盖两缝几何的可分离横向场，不推广至一般二维涡旋。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 采用解析分析结合实验可检验性论证：分析φ^*ψ在移动零点附近所需电流速度的发散阶数（距离的-2k次方）、Doob h-变换与Bernstein条件的节点失效、弱动量分解为有界电流部分与发散渗透部分，并通过两缝实验的条纹可见度指标将该数学结果连接到可观测数据。

Abstract: Quantum Schrödinger bridge constructions establish the existence of a conditioned process under a positivity hypothesis on the endpoint data, and three independent ones adopt it. We show that hypothesis fails on a definite locus once the conditioned object is the two-state amplitude $φ^*ψ$ of a pre- and post-selected pair on configuration space, and that the failure is testable on recorded data. Whenever $φ^*ψ$ has a moving zero of order $k$ across which probability flows, no diffusion of constant diffusion coefficient can both carry the conditioned density and avoid the nodal curve: the current velocity it would need grows as the inverse $2k$-th power of the distance to that curve and points toward it on one side, placing the curve at finite scale distance, so it is reached and not merely approached. Identifying the weak momentum with a bridge drift fails independently: for a free particle post-selected in position the current velocity is exactly minus the drift of the bridge to the target. The positive Doob $h$-transform and the Bernstein conditioning built on it exist wherever $φ^*ψ$ is nodeless and fail on the nodal curve. There the osmotic part of the weak momentum diverges as the inverse distance to the zero and changes sign across it with a coefficient set by $k$, while the current part stays bounded; that boundedness rests on the signed factorization of the real-envelope class and hides the obstruction from any picture built on the current velocity alone. A measured fringe visibility fixes a single length, which puts a Lorentzian of order $10^{-3}$ into the current channel reconstructed in the two-slit trajectory experiment if the contrast is limited by an off-axis zero, and nothing there if by incoherence. The results are one-dimensional, covering the separable transverse field of a two-slit geometry, not general two-dimensional vortices.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Pseudorandomness is increasingly recognized as a key property of ensembles in quantum information theory, statistical mechanics, and quantum many-body physics. Yet it appears in two conceptually different forms: statistical pseudorandomness, embodied by unitary designs, and computational pseudorandomness captured by pseudorandom unitaries (PRUs). The relationship between these two forms of pseudorandomness remains surprisingly poorly understood. Existing PRU constructions reveal this interplay where a statistically randomizing ingredient, a unitary design, is combined with classical cryptographic primitives to produce computational pseudorandomness. We show that statistical pseudorandomness is not necessary for computationally pseudorandom unitaries. We do this by replacing the unitary $2$-design layer in the existing constructions with ensembles that are not even state $1$-designs, yet are sufficiently {\em distinct}, a property we identify to be necessary for any PRU. This yields new non-adaptively secure PRU ensembles whose computational pseudorandomness is obtained without an underlying statistically pseudorandom quantum ensemble, such as a $2$-design. We characterize distinctness via an entangled analogue of anticoncentration and use it to show that distinctness already captures constraints on coherence and imaginarity of PRUs, while identifying broad classes of inputs for which the latter obstruction disappears, enabling real-valued PRUs even for certain (maximally) entangled states. As an application, we use lack of distinctness to constrain the conjectured pseudorandomness of the random phase-Hadamard ensemble to form a PRU.

Abstract: We introduce a diagnostic framework based on the full probability distribution of quantum coherence to probe single-particle and many-body phases at finite temperature. We first apply this approach to the localization transition in the non-interacting Aubry-André model and then extend the analysis to its interacting counterpart, whose phase diagram is substantially richer. We show that the moments of the coherence distribution provide clear signatures of quantum phase transitions and capture the emergence of many-body phases. More importantly, we find that the entropy of the coherence distribution emerges as an even more powerful indicator, retaining clear signatures of the underlying quantum phases while remaining remarkably robust against increasing thermal fluctuations.

Abstract: In this work, we extend the scattering quantum walk (SQW) framework to a lattice of energy-dependent point interactions. This yields, within the coined quantum walk (CQW) formalism, a coin operator that is directly related to the scattering matrix of zero-range potentials. The model thus provides a discrete-time quantum-walk (DTQW) analog of a periodic array of point interactions of the Kronig-Penney type, where the walker's wavenumber serves as a continuous, physically transparent control parameter for the coin operation. We analyze the spectra and the dynamics of position probability and entanglement, yielding distinct results for specific energies and point interactions. We relate the spectral structure to the spatial probability distribution and explicitly characterize the long-time entanglement behavior for each point interaction. The transmission modulus determines the quasienergy gap, bandwidth, and maximum group velocity, while also controlling the long-time coin-position entanglement for the initial state considered. The four families of one-dimensional point interactions ($δ$, $δ'$, crossed and asymmetric) realize qualitatively distinct transmission profiles and span the full range of behavior, including enhanced or strongly suppressed spreading and oscillatory entanglement.

Motivation: 在容错量子计算发展的进程中，大规模模拟包含非Clifford门的量子纠错电路至关重要，但现有模拟方法在规模和门类型上存在限制，需要更高效的技术方案。

Abstract: Simulating quantum error correction (QEC) circuits including non-Clifford gates at scale is important to accelerate progress toward fault-tolerant quantum computing. Here we demonstrate that matrix product state (MPS) techniques can handle many QEC circuits exactly and without restriction on gate types. Crucially, we find that MPS efficiency depends sensitively on implementation choices, and we introduce a series of targeted optimizations that reduce bond dimensions and simulation time by several orders of magnitude compared to naive approaches. We illustrate this with examples including: (a) a rotated surface code quantum memory up to distance 11, (b) logical Bell-state preparation up to distance 9, (c) a 15-to-1 magic-state distillation circuit including hundreds of QEC rounds that we optimize to be simulated with only 11 logical qubits (187 physical qubits) and a maximal bond dimension of 64 in under 40 seconds, and (d) a narrow, deep random circuit that scales linearly with the number of T gates. These results demonstrate the importance of circuit-level optimizations and position MPS as a valuable complement to near-Clifford simulators for QEC circuits.

Abstract: We generalize fractalization, a procedure for the construction of fracton models, from space to spacetime. In this context, the no-strings rule that characterizes type-II fractons leads to a superlinear scaling of the floquet code fault-distance with time, potentially lowering the time overhead required for quantum error correction.

Result: 证明了Haar典型距离和直径均渐近于(π/√3)√Q_D（在窗口M≪Q_D≪D^{8/3}M^{-4/3}内）；选取Q_D=κD^{8/3}M^{-4/3}后，得到无限悬崖Carnot--Carathéodory距离的Haar典型下界为D^{4/3}M^{-2/3}量级，例外集合指数小；由此推导无限悬崖直径的指数下界至少为4/3，否证了Brown的指数一猜想，并给出固定误差无辅助两比特量子电路下界。

Conclusion: 本文显著推进了量子电路度量的几何理解，否证了Brown猜想，建立了Haar典型距离与度量的精确渐近行为，并为无辅助量子电路的下界提供了新结果。

Abstract: Let $D=2^n$ and $M=3n+9\binom n2$. We study the right-invariant one-step-cliff metric $d_Q$ on $\operatorname{PU}(D)$, with unit penalty on Pauli weights one and two and penalty $Q$ on all higher weights. If $M/Q_D\to0$ and $MQ_D^{3/4}/D^2\to0$, then, for every fixed $0<x<π/\sqrt3$, \[
  μ_D\bigl(B_{Q_D}([I],x\sqrt{Q_D})\bigr)\le e^{-c_xD^2}. \] Here $μ_D$ is normalized Haar measure. Thus the Haar-typical distance from the identity and the diameter are both asymptotic to $(π/\sqrt3)\sqrt{Q_D}$ throughout the window $M\ll Q_D\ll D^{8/3}M^{-4/3}$. Choosing $Q_D=κD^{8/3}M^{-4/3}$ with sufficiently small fixed $κ>0$ yields a Haar-typical lower bound of order $D^{4/3}M^{-2/3}$ for the corresponding infinite-cliff Carnot--Carathéodory distance, outside an $e^{-Ω(D^2)}$ exceptional set. The infinite-cliff diameter therefore has exponential lower rate at least $4/3$, disproving Brown's exponent-one conjecture. The same estimate gives a fixed-error no-ancilla two-qubit circuit lower bound of the same order.

Abstract: Coherent light-matter interfaces controllably modifying the state of a photon upon interaction with a stationary qubit are a key resource for implementing deterministic entangling gates for optical quantum technologies. This requires a one-to-one mapping between the state of the scattered photon and that of the embedded qubit. Here, we present an experimental signature of such a bijection by leveraging the spin-induced Kerr rotation present in a low-noise charged quantum dot-micropillar cavity device. Through time-resolved polarization measurements, we project the electron spin to one of its eigenstates with $95\pm2\%$ fidelity with a single reflected photon detection, and follow the subsequent spin relaxation through the detection of a second reflected photon. We demonstrate that, after a transient regime governed by the trion radiative lifetime, two orthogonal polarization states can be produced, each associated to a given spin eigenstate. While the current results are limited by a timescale competition between electron spin relaxation and trion radiative lifetime, they could be improved using hole spins displaying increased relaxation times. Our work paves the way towards deterministic logic gates exploiting this one-to-one mapping between a spin and the polarization of a scattered photon.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 作者用凸包对偶（convex-roof duality）替代不可用的胶合论证，并结合一个与维度无关的厄米算子插值结果（dimension-independent interpolation result for Hermitian operators）来完成证明。

Result: 证明了平方根可分量子最优输运成本（与反对称子空间投影相关）是密度矩阵上的真正距离，从而建立了三角不等式；该结果也证明了相应可分SWAP保真度派生距离的度量性。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了量子最优输运框架中度量性质证明的关键空白，为可分量子最优输运及其相关SWAP保真度构造提供了严格的数学基础。

Abstract: We prove that the square root of the separable quantum optimal transport cost associated with the orthogonal projection onto the antisymmetric subspace defines a genuine distance between density matrices. Equivalently, this establishes the triangle inequality for the order-two Beatty-França quantum optimal transport construction induced by the Hilbert-Schmidt distance between pure states. The result also proves metricity of the corresponding distance derived from separable SWAP fidelity. The proof replaces the unavailable gluing argument by convex-roof duality and a dimension-independent interpolation result for Hermitian operators.

</details>


### [98] [Experimental validation of a compact fault-tolerant architecture for trapped ions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03194)
*Noah Berthusen,Ali Lavasani,Asmae Benhemou,M. S. Allman,Joan Dreiling,Brian Estey,Cameron Foltz,Trent Jacobs,Michael Mills,Annie Jihyun Park,Adam P. Reed,David Hayes,Tzvetan S. Metodi,Andrew C. Potter*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文介绍并实验验证了一种基于[[20,2,6]] C₄-Helix码的容错量子计算架构，在Quantinuum Helios 98比特离子阱量子处理器上实现了重复量子纠错、完整两逻辑量子比特的Clifford群运算以及C₄-Helix与距离5表面码之间的容错链式映射接口，编码实现均优于对应的无编码物理基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 采用[[20,2,6]] C₄-Helix码设计面向早期容错时代的量子计算架构，在Quantinuum Helios 98比特离子阱处理器上实验验证其核心组件：重复量子纠错、完整两逻辑量子比特Clifford群基准测试、以及C₄-Helix与距离5表面码的容错链式映射接口以制备异构三逻辑量子比特GHZ态。

Conclusion: 实验结果确立了C₄-Helix码作为一种经过硬件验证的容错体系架构，而非仅仅是一个量子存储器。

Abstract: Quantum error correction (QEC) is beginning to enable logical operations that outperform their unencoded physical counterparts, but useful fault-tolerant computation will require more than low-error quantum memory. An effective architecture must orchestrate efficient logical encoding, low-overhead logical operations, and access to the non-Clifford resources required for universal computation. Here, we introduce and experimentally validate such an architecture based on the $[[20,2,6]]$ $C_4$-Helix code, designed for the early fault-tolerant regime. Using Quantinuum Helios, a 98-qubit trapped-ion quantum processor, we experimentally demonstrate the principal components of this architecture: we perform repeated quantum error correction with an error of $4.6^{+6.2}_{-2.6}\times10^{-5}$ per logical qubit per QEC cycle. We benchmark the complete Clifford group on the two logical qubits of a single codeblock under active error correction, obtaining an error of $2.8^{+1.0}_{-1.6}\times 10^{-4}$ per two-qubit logical Clifford. We further demonstrate a fault-tolerant chain-map interface between $C_4$-Helix and a distance-5 surface code, preparing a heterogeneous three-logical-qubit GHZ state with a fidelity lower bound of $99.925^{+0.068}_{-0.245}\%$. In each case, the encoded implementation outperforms its corresponding unencoded physical baseline without relying on postselection. Circuit-level simulations indicate that improvements in physical fidelity bring the same architecture into the $10^{-6}$-$10^{-8}$ logical-error regime targeted for early fault-tolerant computation. Together, these results establish $C_4$-Helix as a hardware-validated fault-tolerant architecture rather than a bare quantum memory.

</details>


### [99] [Discretization-Aware Fine-Tuning for Quantum Machine Learning with Chemical Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03220)
*Shunji Matsuura,Sonika Johri*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出DAFT（离散化感知微调）方法，通过对预训练化学基础模型进行微调，使输出表征在量化为离散比特串后仍保持信息量，从而降低量子机器学习中跨类碰撞概率。在BBBP分子性质预测任务上，DAFT将碰撞计数降低数个量级，量子分类准确率提升超过12个百分点；在10比特设置下，量子模型（0.883）超越匹配的经典基线（0.855，p=0.026），展示了信息受限场景下表示与量子编码对齐对实现量子优势的关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近量子设备将高维经典数据编码到小型量子寄存器时存在严重的容量瓶颈，导致基于比特编码的输入产生跨类碰撞——不同标签的样本映射到相同离散比特串而无法被下游模型区分。这说明需要改善数据表示本身，使其在离散量化后仍保持判别信息。

Method: 提出DAFT（discretization-aware fine-tuning）方法，通过可微的软碰撞损失函数，针对预训练的化学基础模型（ChemBERTa-77M）进行微调，使生成的表征在量化编码（basis-encoded bit-bit）后降低碰撞概率、保留足够的类别判别信息。实验设置中量子与经典模型接收完全相同的离散比特串输入，以隔离表示效果与模型架构的影响。

Result: 在BBBP基准上，DAFT使碰撞数量下降数个数量级，量子分类准确率较冻结骨干提升超过12个百分点。无DAFT时经典模型在相同输入约束下优于QML；启用DAFT后，在10比特下量子模型以0.883对0.855（p=0.026）超越匹配的经典基线。

Conclusion: 在信息受限（离散编码）的量子机器学习场景中，获得量子优势的关键在于将连续特征表示与离散量子编码方式对齐。本文表明表示层面的对齐（而非模型架构本身）是决定QML能否在分类任务中胜过经典模型的核心因素。

Abstract: A key challenge in practical quantum machine learning (QML), particularly for discriminative tasks such as classification, is the limited capacity of near-term quantum devices to encode high-dimensional classical data into small quantum registers. In optimized basis-encoded (bit-bit) settings, this constraint leads to cross-class collisions, where samples with different labels are mapped to the same discrete bit-string and thus become indistinguishable to any downstream model. In this work, we investigate how data representation affects QML performance under such severe information bottlenecks. We introduce discretization-aware fine-tuning (DAFT), a method that adapts a pre-trained chemical foundation model to produce representations that remain informative after quantization. DAFT reduces collision probability through a differentiable soft collision loss. We evaluate both quantum and classical models under a controlled setting in which they receive identical discretized bit-string inputs, isolating the effect of representation from model architecture. On the blood-brain barrier penetration (BBBP) molecular property prediction benchmark using ChemBERTa-77M, DAFT reduces collision counts by several orders of magnitude and improves quantum classification accuracy by more than 12 percentage points compared to a frozen backbone. Importantly, without DAFT, classical models outperform QML under the same input constraints. With DAFT, however, this comparison reverses at higher qubit counts. At 10 qubits, the quantum model surpasses a matched classical baseline trained on identical bit-strings (0.883 vs. 0.855, $p = 0.026$). These results show that, in information-constrained regimes, achieving a quantum advantage critically depends on aligning continuous representations with discrete quantum encodings.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散时间晶体是一种非平衡物质相，其特征是离散时间平移对称性的自发破缺。作者旨在探究在强无序自旋链中（包括各向同性Heisenberg相互作用点）是否存在离散时间晶体行为，并系统评估其稳定性。

Method: 采用基于矩阵乘积态（MPS）的大规模数值模拟方法，研究强无序的Heisenberg相互作用自旋链在周期驱动下的动力学。从多体局域化（MBL）区域出发，施加δ脉冲旋转，通过纠缠熵、量子Fisher信息、短程自旋关联和限制控制遍历功等多种可观测量，系统考察旋转角误差对次谐波响应稳定性的影响。

Result: 观测到在驱动频率一半处自旋观测量呈现显著的次谐波响应。增大旋转误差时，发现一个介于时间晶体响应和Floquet局域化响应之间的中间动力学区域，该区域中大多数观测量表现出类似Anderson局域化的弱关联动力学特征。

Conclusion: 研究为强无序Heisenberg自旋链在周期驱动下存在离散时间晶态行为提供了证据，并揭示了一个连接时间晶态与Floquet局域化的中间动力学区域，深化了对时间晶体稳定性及其退相干机制的理解。

Abstract: A discrete time crystal is an out-of-equilibrium phase of matter characterized by the spontaneous breaking of discrete time-translation symmetry. Using extensive numerical simulations based on matrix-product-state methods, we provide evidence for discrete time-crystalline behavior in strongly disordered spin chains with Heisenberg interactions, including the isotropic point, subject to periodic driving. Starting from a many-body localized regime, we observe that rotations induced by delta kicks produce a pronounced subharmonic response at half the drive frequency in spin observables. We investigate the stability of this response against rotation-angle errors through entanglement entropy, quantum Fisher information, short-range spin correlations, and restricted-control ergotropy. Increasing the rotation error reveals an intermediate dynamical regime separating the time-crystalline and Floquet-localized responses. In this regime, most observables exhibit signatures of weakly correlated dynamics reminiscent of Anderson localization.

</details>


### [101] [Quantum Complexity in Nuclear Scattering and Fission Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03240)
*Saurabh V. Kadam,Antonio Bjelčić,Nicolas Schunck,Kyle Wendt*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究了核反应动力学中的量子复杂性指标（双粒子纠缠熵和非局域魔法/非稳定化子），通过分析一维强相互作用费米子散射模型（Negele势）和240Pu裂变的TDHFB真实模拟，发现两种过程都动态生成显著的纠缠和非局域魔法，表明量子计算机在精确模拟核反应动力学方面具有实质优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算机有望模拟经典计算机无法处理的强关联量子多体系统（如原子核），但实现这一潜力需要首先理解目标问题的量子复杂性结构。作者旨在通过量化核反应动力学中的量子复杂性指标，评估量子计算在此类问题上的潜在优势。

Method: 作者追踪了两种量子复杂性指标的时演化：双粒子纠缠熵和非局域魔法（非稳定化子）。分析了两个代表性动力学过程：(1) 一维强相互作用费米子散射模型（由Negele势控制）；(2) 在含时Hartree-Fock-Bogoliubov (TDHFB)理论框架下对240Pu裂变的现实模拟。

Result: (1) 在一维散射过程中，相互作用动态地同时产生了纠缠和非局域魔法，并在出射态中留下量子复杂性的持久特征；(2) 在240Pu裂变模拟中，观察到断点（scission）之后，子碎片的空间二分中仍保留了显著的量子复杂性。两种情况下均检测到可观的非局域魔法和纠缠。

Conclusion: 两种核反应动力学过程中存在的显著非局域魔法和纠缠，强烈表明量子计算机将在精确模拟核反应动力学方面提供实质性优势。

Abstract: Quantum computers promise advantages for simulating strongly correlated quantum many-body systems, like atomic nuclei, that are beyond the reach of classical computers. Realizing this potential requires understanding the quantum complexity structure of the target problem. We investigate the time-evolution of two key indicators of quantum complexity, bipartite entanglement entropy and non-local magic (non-stabilizerness), in nuclear reaction dynamics. We analyze two representative dynamical processes: scattering in a one-dimensional model of strongly interacting fermions governed by the Negele potential, and a realistic simulation of $^{240}$Pu fission within time-dependent Hartree-Fock-Bogoliubov (TDHFB) theory. In the former case, we find that interactions dynamically generate both entanglement and non-local magic, leaving persistent signatures of quantum complexity in the outgoing states. In the latter, we observe that substantial quantum complexity survives in the spatial bipartition of daughter fragments well beyond scission. The presence of significant non-local magic and entanglement in both cases strongly indicate that quantum computers would provide substantial advantages for accurately simulating nuclear reaction dynamics.

</details>


### [102] [Gravitationally Induced Entanglement Across an Event Horizon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04104)
*Hatim Salih*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在社会引力推迟势模型下证明，粒子穿越黑洞事件视界后仍可与其外部量子纠缠态从头建立纠缠，但径向提取需非惯性减速并引发软引力子轫致辐射，从而建立退相干下界Gamma>=729/(160π)Phi；而宏观光学质量通过切向量子擦除可令纠缠态横向遥传至无穷远，显露出时空关于纠缠退化的几何对偶性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统假设认为一旦粒子跨过黑洞事件视界且与外部粒子不纠缠，就无法再与该粒子动态纠缠。本文旨在挑战这一假定，探索视界两侧粒子能否经由引力推迟效应从零建立纠缠，并考察其可提取性与背后的几何结构。

Method: 采用引力推迟势（gravitational retarded-potential）模型进行分析。具体地：1) 考虑跨越事件视界的自由下坠空间叠加态粒子；2) 分析其径向提取所需的非惯性减速过程，计算由此触发的软引力子轫致辐射；3) 推导纠缠相与去相干之间的严格下界关系；4) 作为对照，构造等效宏观光学质量系统进行局部切向纠缠采集（量子擦除方案），以揭示两种路径的几何对偶。

Result: 研究发现纠缠确实可以在跨越视界的粒子对之间从头建立，打破传统假设。但径向提取会让系统经历非惯性减速，从而引发软引力子轫致辐射，并导致严格的去相干下界Gamma >= 729/(160pi)Phi，最终使纠缠态退相干。与之对比，宏观光学质量等效系统可经切向量子擦除在局部采集纠缠，实现纠缠态的横向遥传。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了时空几何中的一个深刻对偶性：当局域化质量被从视界拖离时，时空不可逆地降解纠缠；然而沿横向（切向）方向，纠缠态却可被遥传至无穷远处。这暗示事件视界附近的纠缠动力学不仅受限于因果结构，还与其提取路径的几何方向密切相关。

Abstract: It has long been assumed that a particle---having crossed a black hole event horizon unentangled with another particle in the exterior universe---can no longer dynamically entangle with that particle. Here, we demonstrate within a gravitational retarded-potential model that entanglement can in fact be created from scratch between freely falling spatial superpositions across an event horizon. Yet, extracting this state radially requires non-inertial deceleration, triggering soft-graviton bremsstrahlung, and establishing a strict dephasing bound in terms of entangling phase, $Γ\ge\frac{729}{160π}Φ$. This decoheres the entangled state. By contrast, equivalent macroscopic optical masses allow local tangential harvesting of entanglement via quantum erasure, thus revealing a remarkable geometric duality: Spacetime irreversibly degrades entanglement the moment the localized mass is dragged away from the horizon, while allowing the transverse teleportation of its entangled state to infinity.

</details>


### [103] [Quantum thermalization achieves optimal approximate quantum error correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04121)
*Aditi Venkatesh,Richard R. Allen,Saúl Pilatowsky-Cameo,Bingtian Ye,Soonwon Choi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将量子纠错的严格框架移植到量子热化研究中，揭示热化动力学在典型晚期时间状态下天然形成近似量子纠错码。数值发现编码率、距离与热熵密度之间存在普适关系，该关系在无限温度下饱和量子Singleton界，在有限温度下通过Scrooge系综构造的编码族饱和熵量子Singleton界，表明热化本身具有最优编码结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子热化（孤立多体系统自然演化至热态）与量子纠错在机制上存在深层关联——二者都涉及信息在局域测量的缺失与保护。以往对热化的理论框架缺乏严格的纠错理论视角，作者希望利用纠错理论的严谨性来刻画热化过程的编码能力，建立两者统一的定量描述。

Method: 将典型晚期热化态视为码字（codewords），借用（近似）量子纠错理论框架分析热化动力学。通过数值方法研究一般热化动力学的纠错性质，提取编码率、距离与热熵密度之间的普适关系。在无限温度下与Haar随机码比较，在有限温度下引入基于Scrooge系综（Haar系综的热类似物）的编码族，并利用熵量子Singleton界限制证明其最优性。进一步分析守恒量对热化纠错行为的影响，考察不同能量或守恒荷码字的信息泄漏与可纠错性的阈值。

Result: (1) 数值发现热化动力学中编码率、距离与热熵密度之间存在普适曲线；(2) 无限温度下该曲线饱和量子Singleton界，达到与Haar随机码相同的极限；(3) 有限温度下引入的Scrooge系综编码族被证明饱和熵量子Singleton界，是最优编码族；(4) 实验中提取的普适曲线独立饱和同一界限，说明有限温度热化本身具有最优性；(5) 守恒量限制热化的纠错能力：不同能量或守恒荷的码字仅泄漏经典信息，可纠错性维持到能量差达到热涨落尺度为止。

Conclusion: 热化动力学蕴含普适的最优编码结构，与量子纠错理论中的基本极限（量子Singleton界、熵量子Singleton界）精确吻合。这一发现不仅从纠错视角深化了对量子热化的理解，还引入了新的最优量子码家族（基于Scrooge系综），推进了近似量子纠错理论在有限温度场景下的边界。

Abstract: Quantum thermalization explains how an isolated many-body system naturally evolves towards a thermal state, rendering information about the initial conditions inaccessible to local measurements. This is precisely the mechanism utilized in quantum error correction, where information is protected by design through a nonlocal encoding. In this work, we leverage this connection to port the rigorous framework of (approximate) quantum error correction to the study of quantum thermalization. Treating typical late-time states as codewords, we characterize the error-correcting properties of generic thermalizing dynamics. We numerically uncover a universal relationship between the encoding rate, distance, and thermal entropy density of the emergent code. At infinite temperature, this universal curve saturates the quantum Singleton bound, achieving the same optimal limit as Haar-random codes. At finite temperature, we introduce a code family based on the Scrooge ensemble, the natural thermal analogue of the Haar ensemble, and prove it saturates the entropic quantum Singleton bound, establishing this family as optimal within entropic constraints. Our extracted universal curve independently saturates this same bound, revealing that finite-temperature thermalization is itself optimal. Finally, we show how conserved quantities limit the error-correcting behavior of thermalization: codewords with differing energies, or other conserved charges, leak only classical information, and correctability persists until the difference reaches the scale of thermal fluctuations. Our results reveal a universal optimal coding structure in thermalizing dynamics, while introducing new optimal codes that achieve fundamental limits of approximate quantum error correction.

</details>


### [104] [Quantum Vacuum Nonlinearities in Laser Interferometers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03314)
*Zain Mehdi,Joseph J. Hope,Simon A. Haine*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出使用驻波激光干涉仪进行全光学光子-光子相互作用测试，无需外部磁场和常规激光源即可在真空中探测量子电动力学预测的光子-光子散射信号，灵敏度随腔体内循环功率非线性提升，并可扩展到探测超越标准模型的量子场性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子电动力学预测真空中存在光子-光子散射等非线性量子现象，但此前探测方案通常依赖强外部磁场或极高端设备。本文旨在提出一种无需外部磁场、仅使用常规激光器的全光学方案，使该实验在实验室尺度内可行，并能为探测超越标准模型的物理提供新途径。

Method: 利用驻波激光干涉仪作为探测平台。光子-光子散射会改变干涉仪中光场的相位或偏振状态，从而产生可测量的干涉信号。通过提高腔体内的循环功率，信号的灵敏度呈非线性提升，从而增强信噪比。该实验框架还可被改用于探测标准模型之外的新量子场效应。

Result: 理论上证明基于驻波干涉仪的全光学方案可达到探测QED光子-光子散射所需的灵敏度，且无需外部磁场；实验规模可行于实验室建造，灵敏度可持续循环功率非线性增长；该架构同样适用于超越标准模型的物理探测。

Conclusion: 全光学干涉测量为探测真空非线性电动力学行为提供了可行且可扩展的路径，既验证QED基础预言，也为搜寻新物理提供了新的实验工具。

Abstract: We propose all-optical tests of photon-photon interactions in the matter vacuum using standing-wave laser interferometers, which do not require external magnetic fields and use conventional laser sources. We show that the interferometric detection of photon-photon scattering predicted by quantum electrodynamics is within reach of laboratory-scale experiments with a sensitivity that improves nonlinearly with the circulating power within the cavity. We outline how such an experiment could be adapted to probe properties of quantum fields beyond the Standard Model of particle physics.

</details>


### [105] [Efficient Estimation of Reduced QAOA Expressibility on Acyclic Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03317)
*Bao Bach,Boris Tsvelikhovskiy,Jose Falla,Ilya Safro*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究对称约化的QAOA在树状图上的动力学李代数（DLA），提出一种多项式时间的经典算法，仅利用图的顶点区分过程（基于最短路径结构和度奇偶性）即可高效获取约化DLA的关键信息，包括在完全分辨时确定完整DLA并验证最大表达能力，在部分分辨时识别嵌入子代数并给出DLA维数的下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MaxCut问题中，经典等价的优化问题表述在量子算法中未必等价。对单个顶点固定值可去除全局对称性且不改变优化问题，但会显著改变QAOA的量子动力学。这些动力学由电路的DLA刻画，而DLA的直接构造可能指数级昂贵，因此需要一种高效的经典方法来分析约化DLA。

Method: 提出一种递归区分顶点的多项式经典算法，利用最短路径结构和度奇偶性对树上的顶点进行分类。当所有顶点被逐一分辨时，可完全确定约化DLA并证明QAOA ansatz的最大可表达性；否则可识别嵌入子代数、提供DLA维数的严格下界并验证可控制子系统。

Result: 理论分析和实验结果表明，该算法能仅从图结构高效诊断对称约化QAOA的量子动力学，在完全分辨率下确定完整约化DLA，在非完全分辨率下仍能提供有意义的代数结构信息。

Conclusion: 经典图结构可以在量子硬件运行之前用于诊断乃至指导变分算法的量子动力学，为理解和设计高效的QAOA提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: Classically equivalent formulations of an optimization problem need not lead to equivalent quantum algorithms. For MaxCut, fixing the value of a single vertex removes a simple global symmetry without changing the underlying optimization problem, yet it can substantially alter the quantum dynamics generated by the Quantum Approximate Optimization Algorithm (QAOA). These dynamics are captured by the circuit's dynamical Lie algebra (DLA), whose direct construction can become exponentially expensive. Here we show that, for symmetry-reduced QAOA on trees, substantial information about the reduced DLA can instead be obtained efficiently from the graph alone. We introduce a polynomial classical algorithm that recursively distinguishes vertices using shortest path structure and degree parity. When all vertices are resolved individually, the method determines the complete reduced DLA and, under the corresponding graph conditions, certifies maximal expressibility of the reduced QAOA ansatz. Even when full resolution is not achieved, the algorithm identifies embedded subalgebras, provides rigorous lower bounds on DLA dimension, and certifies controllable subsystems. Our theoretical and experimental results show how classical graph structure can be used to diagnose and potentially guide the quantum dynamics of variational algorithms before running them on quantum hardware.

</details>


### [106] [An interpretation-independent formulation of the measurement problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03409)
*Antoine Soulas*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文不试图解决测量问题，而是以独立于诠释的方式重新表述它。作者指出量子物质无法用Kolmogorov概率论描述，由此将测量问题重构为"无法为量子物质构建独立于认识论的实在论"——即不存在对量子系统的上帝视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测量问题之所以缺乏精确且公认的定义，是因为它在不同量子力学诠释中呈现不同面貌。受Bell定理等理论无关结果的启发，作者希望以独立于诠释的方式识别该难题的共同根源，仅从经验统计的性质出发进行讨论。

Method: 作者的核心论点是量子物质无法由Kolmogorovian（柯尔莫哥洛夫）概率论描述。通过论证Kolmogorov概率公理正是认知不确定性（epistemic uncertainty）的假设，作者将测量问题重新表述为：为量子物质构建独立于认识论的实在论（本体论）是不可能的。

Result: 该表述将测量问题视为宇宙统计特征而非可解的难题，但带来三方面益处：(i) 为各种诠释的存在提供新视角；(ii) 为量子力学与广义相对论的不相容性提供非纯数学/技术性的根本原因；(iii) 为量子引力研究提供指导方向。

Conclusion: 依此定义，测量问题本身"不可解"因为它并非待解的谜题，而是宇宙统计学的一个根本特征——不存在上帝视角的量子系统。

Abstract: In this paper, we do not try to solve the measurement problem, but rather to properly formulate it. One of the reasons why it still lacks a precise, agreed definition is that the problem may take very different aspects depending on the interpretation of quantum mechanics embraced. Inspired by the methodology of theory-independent results like Bell's theorem, we propose to identify the common root of the puzzle in an interpretation-independent way i.e. as a property of the empirical statistics only, before deriving its philosophical consequences. The key observation is that quantum matter can not be described by a Kolmogorovian probabilistic theory. Arguing that the Kolmogorov axioms of probability theory are the postulates of epistemic uncertainty leads us to reformulate the measurement problem as the impossibility to build an ontology independent of epistemology for quantum matter. Said differently, there exists no God's-eye view on quantum systems. Although it is meaningless to solve the measurement problem as defined in this way, insofar as it is a feature of the universe's statistics, such a formulation may on the other hand bring side benefits. In particular, we argue that it allows to: (i) shed a new light on the variety of interpretations; (ii) propose a fundamental reason why quantum mechanics and general relativity are so incompatible, not relying on purely mathematical or technical arguments; (iii) guide the quest for quantum gravity.

</details>


### [107] [Redesigning quantum theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03333)
*Matthew B. Weiss*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文档围绕QBism（量子贝叶斯主义）展开，主张量子力学本质上是概率论加上额外的非经典相干条件。论文从第一性原理出发发展这些相干条件，证明QBist框架与广义概率论（GPT）框架等价，并指出非经典相干关系几乎总可视为全概率律的温和修正。当参考测量对应复数射影3-设计时，量子态空间的形状隐含于参考测量本身的概率之中，从而仅凭一个参考测量的相干就蕴含整个有限维量子力学的相干。论文还给出一个温和的量子理论重建方法，其实际后果是提供了一种理论无关的、自检验复数射影t-设计（t≥3）的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
